  <div class="popup-content">
    
<form class="search-form" role="search" method="get" id="" action="https://www.group-ib.com/">
  <input type="search" placeholder="Search" name="s" id="search-input" value=""/>
  <button type="submit" class="search-button"><img src="/wp-content/themes/gib-theme/assets/images/search-icon.svg" alt="Search Icon"></button>
</form>   </div>

<style>
  .hidden {
    display: none;
  }
</style>

<script>
  const openSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.open-search-popup')];
  const closeSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.close-search-popup')];
  const searchPopup = document.querySelector('.search-popup');

  const closePopup = () => {
    searchPopup.style.display = 'none';
    openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.add('hidden'));
  };

  openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(openSearchPopupBtn => {
    openSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();

      searchPopup.style.display = 'block';
      openSearchPopupBtn.classList.add('hidden');
      closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    });
  });

  closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(closeSearchPopupBtn => {
    closeSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      closePopup();
    });
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target) && !openSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target)) && !closeSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target))) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target)) {
          closePopup();
        }
      }, 100);
    }
  });
</script>    </div>

                      onFormReady: function ($form) {

                          apiKey: "2c6794ccffb44069aedee95495c7b1d2", // replace YOUR_PUBLIC_API_KEY_HERE with the generated API KEY,
                          disableSubmitOnError: false, // Changed to false to allow form submission even if validation fails

                              // Form will still work without ZeroBounce validation
                            }
                          } else {
                            // Script might still be loading, retry after a short delay
                            setTimeout(initZeroBounce, 100);
                          }
                        }
                        initZeroBounce();
                      }
                    });

  // posts depends on nav link click
  jQuery('.blog__sidebar nav a').click(function () {
    if (jQuery(this).hasClass('active')) {} else {
      jQuery('#load-more-posts').hide();
      postsLoad=5;
      if (jQuery(this).hasClass('all')) {
        postsCount = jQuery(".blog .blog-posts__wrapper a.blogpost-card").length;
        if(postsCount>postsLoad){
          jQuery('#load-more-posts').show();
        }
        history.replaceState(null, null, ' ');
        jQuery('.blog__sidebar nav a').removeClass('active');
        jQuery(this).addClass('active');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper').find("a.first-of-category").removeClass('first-of-category');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper').find("a.blogpost-card:nth-child(1)").addClass('first-of-category');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper a.blogpost-card').removeClass('show');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper a.blogpost-card:lt('+postsLoad+')').addClass('show');
      } else {
        let urlhash = jQuery(this).attr('id');
        let urlid = urlhash.substring(1);
        postsCount = jQuery(".blog .blog-posts__wrapper a.blogpost-card#" + urlid).length;
        if(postsCount>postsLoad){
          jQuery('#load-more-posts').show();
        }
        history.replaceState(null, null, urlhash);
        jQuery('.blog__sidebar nav a').removeClass('active');
        jQuery(this).addClass('active');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper a.blogpost-card').removeClass('show');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper').find("a.blogpost-card.first-of-category").removeClass('first-of-category');
        let listByCategory = jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper').find("a.blogpost-card#" + urlid);
        jQuery(listByCategory[0]).addClass('first-of-category').addClass('show');
        jQuery('.blog .blog-posts__wrapper a.blogpost-card[id='+urlid+']:lt('+postsLoad+')').addClass('show');
      }
    }
  });

</script>
